C.10 sensitivity.py ——参数、环境与模型形式灵敏度分析
1 # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
2 # ============================================================================
3 # 文件名：sensitivity.py
4 # 作用
：灵敏度与不确定度分析，共四组：
5 #
(1) 物性与传递参数：D 的前置因子、活化温度3850 K、浓度指数、rho、cp、
6 #
k、h、h_m 的单因子扰动（±1%~±10%）；
7 #
(2) 烘房条件：设定温度、设定含湿量、预热/恒温切换时刻、预热时间常数，
8 #
以及用附件1 的原始噪声数据代替拟合曲线；
9 #
(3) 模型形式：是否计入表面蒸发潜热冷却、问题4 是否计入收缩、
10 #
是否计入物质坐标下能量方程的体积变化项、收缩曲线用拟合还是插值；
11 #
(4) 汇总排序，指出主导不确定性的参数。
12 #
所有结果都以「与同一网格基准的差值」给出，以抵消离散误差。
13 # 输出：data/sensitivity.csv、data/sensitivity_summary.txt。
14 #
注：所有run 都用与基准相同的网格与步长安排，因此delta 是有意义的。
15 # ============================================================================
16 """
17 Sensitivity and uncertainty study of the two drying times.
18
19 Groups of runs
20 --------------
21 1. Material / transport parameters: one-at-a-time perturbations of the
22
Appendix 3 and Appendix 4 correlations (pre-exponential factor of D, the
23
activation temperature 3850 K, the concentration exponent, rho, cp, k) and
24
of the transfer coefficients h and h_m.
25 2. Chamber conditions: set-point temperature and moisture, switching time of
26
the constant-temperature stage, and the choice of the preheat model.
27 3. Measured-data noise: the fitted smooth chamber history is replaced by the
28
raw (noisy) Attachment-1 record.
29 4. Model form: shrinkage on/off for problem 4, the volume-change term of the
30
shrinking-frame energy balance, and the evaporative (latent-heat) cooling
31
of the surface.
32
33 Every run reports t_dry relative to the baseline computed with the same grid,
34 so that the discretisation error cancels.
35 """
36
37 import os
38 import sys
39 import io
40 import time
41 import numpy as np
42 import pandas as pd
43
44 if sys.stdout.encoding is None or "utf" not in sys.stdout.encoding.lower():
45
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding="utf-8",
46
errors="replace")
73

===== PAGE 74 =====
47 sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
48 import common as cm
49
50 C_TARGET = 0.15
51 T_END = 2.6e5
52 N_CELL = 400
53 SCHED = [(7200.0, 1.0), (T_END, 10.0)]
54 T_REP = np.arange(1.0, T_END + 0.5, 30.0)
55 XI_REP = np.array([0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0])
56
57
58 # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
59 def props23_scaled(A=2.4e-3, Ea=3850.0, b=0.45, rho0=650.0, rho1=128.0,
60
cp0=1450.0, cp1=2736.0, k0=0.21, k1=0.38):
61
"""Appendix 3 correlations with every constant exposed for perturbation."""
62
def prop(C, T_K):
63
C = np.maximum(np.asarray(C, dtype=float), cm.C_FLOOR)
64
f = C / (C + 1.0)
65
return (rho0 + rho1 * C, cp0 + cp1 * f, k0 + k1 * f,
66
A * np.exp(-b / C) * np.exp(-Ea / T_K))
67
return prop
68
69
70 def props4_scaled(A=4.2e-4, Ea=3850.0, b=0.30, rho0=760.0, rho1=90.0,
71
cp0=1850.0, cp1=2150.0, k0=0.12, k1=0.20):
72
"""Appendix 4 correlations with every constant exposed for perturbation."""
73
def prop(C, T_K):
74
C = np.maximum(np.asarray(C, dtype=float), cm.C_FLOOR)
75
f = C / (C + 1.0)
76
return (rho0 + rho1 * C, cp0 + cp1 * f, k0 + k1 * f,
77
A * np.exp(-b / C) * np.exp(-Ea / T_K))
78
return prop
79
80
81 def drying_time(prop, radius=None, room=None, grid="node", n_cell=N_CELL,
82
h_conv=None, hm_conv=None, volume_term=False,
83
evap_cooling=False):
84
room = room if room is not None else cm.RoomConditions()
85
res = cm.simulate(n_cell, prop, T_END, 1.0, room, radius=radius, grid=grid,
86
h_conv=h_conv, hm_conv=hm_conv, volume_term=volume_term,
87
evap_cooling=evap_cooling, dt_schedule=SCHED,
88
t_report=T_REP, xi_report=XI_REP, track_max=True)
89
Cmax = res["Cmax"]
# max over the WHOLE node field
90
idx = np.where(Cmax < C_TARGET)[0]
91
if not len(idx):
92
return np.nan, res
93
i0 = idx[0]
94
t0, t1 = res["t"][i0 - 1], res["t"][i0]
95
v0, v1 = Cmax[i0 - 1], Cmax[i0]
96
return t0 + (C_TARGET - v0) * (t1 - t0) / (v1 - v0), res
97
98
99 class MeasuredRoom:
100
"""Raw (noisy) Attachment-1 chamber record, held constant at the end."""
101
102
def __init__(self):
103
df = cm.load_attachment1()
104
self.t = df["t"].to_numpy(float)
105
self.T = df["T_air"].to_numpy(float)
106
self.C = df["C_air"].to_numpy(float)
107
108
def T_air(self, t):
109
t = np.asarray(t, dtype=float)
110
v = np.interp(t, self.t, self.T)
111
return np.where(t > self.t[-1], self.T[-1], v) if v.ndim else float(v)
112
74

===== PAGE 75 =====
113
def C_air(self, t):
114
t = np.asarray(t, dtype=float)
115
v = np.interp(t, self.t, self.C)
116
return np.where(t > self.t[-1], self.C[-1], v) if v.ndim else float(v)

119 def main():
120
rad = cm.RadiusHistory(mode="table")
121
out = []
122
P = lambda s: (print(s, flush=True), out.append(s))
123
rows = []
124
125
P("=" * 84)
126
P("SENSITIVITY OF THE DRYING TIME
(n=%d, dt = 1 s -> 10 s, t_end = %.0f s)"
127
% (N_CELL, T_END))
128
P("=" * 84)
129
130
base = {}
131
for tag, prop, radius in [("P3", props23_scaled(), None),
132
("P4", props4_scaled(), rad)]:
133
t0 = time.time()
134
td, _ = drying_time(prop, radius=radius)
135
base[tag] = td
136
P("
baseline %s : t_dry = %.1f s = %.4f h
(%.1f s wall)"
137
% (tag, td, td / 3600.0, time.time() - t0))
138
P("")
139
P("
%-6s %-28s %-10s %-14s %-12s" %
140
("case", "perturbed quantity", "change", "t_dry / h", "delta / h"))
141
142
def run(tag, label, change, prop, radius, **kw):
143
td, _ = drying_time(prop, radius=radius, **kw)
144
d = td - base[tag]
145
P("
%-6s %-28s %-10s %-14.4f %+12.4f" %
146
(tag, label, change, td / 3600.0, d / 3600.0))
147
rows.append(dict(case=tag, quantity=label, change=change,
148
t_dry_h=td / 3600.0, delta_h=d / 3600.0))
149
return d
150
151
# ---- 1. material and transport parameters -------------------------
152
for tag, radius, factory in [("P3", None, props23_scaled),
153
("P4", rad, props4_scaled)]:
154
for pct in (-0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02):
155
run(tag, "D0 pre-exponential", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
156
factory(A=(2.4e-3 if tag == "P3" else 4.2e-4) * (1 + pct)),
157
radius=radius)
158
for pct in (-0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02):

run(tag, "activation T (3850 K)", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
160
factory(Ea=3850.0 * (1 + pct)), radius=radius)
161
for pct in (-0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02):
162
run(tag, "concentration exponent", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
163
factory(b=(0.45 if tag == "P3" else 0.30) * (1 + pct)),
164
radius=radius)
165
for pct in (-0.02, 0.02):
166
run(tag, "rho correlation", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
167
factory(rho0=(650.0 if tag == "P3" else 760.0) * (1 + pct),
168
rho1=(128.0 if tag == "P3" else 90.0) * (1 + pct)),
169
radius=radius)
170
run(tag, "cp correlation", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
171
factory(cp0=(1450.0 if tag == "P3" else 1850.0) * (1 + pct),
172
cp1=(2736.0 if tag == "P3" else 2150.0) * (1 + pct)),
173
radius=radius)
174
run(tag, "k correlation", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
175
factory(k0=(0.21 if tag == "P3" else 0.12) * (1 + pct),
176
k1=(0.38 if tag == "P3" else 0.20) * (1 + pct)),
177
radius=radius)
178
for pct in (-0.10, -0.05, 0.05, 0.10):
75

===== PAGE 76 =====
179
run(tag, "h_m (mass transfer)", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),
180
factory(), radius=radius, hm_conv=cm.HM_CONV * (1 + pct))
181
for pct in (-0.10, 0.10):
182
run(tag, "h (heat transfer)", "%+.0f%%" % (100 * pct),

factory(), radius=radius, h_conv=cm.H_CONV * (1 + pct))

P("")

radius=radius, room=cm.RoomConditions(t_set=cm.T_SET_C + dT))

radius=radius,

201
radius=radius,

room=cm.RoomConditions(tau_t=cm.TAU_AIR_T * tau,

tau_c=cm.TAU_AIR_C * tau))
204
run(tag, "no preheat ramp (T_air=28)", "alt", factory(),

radius=radius,

room=cm.RoomConditions(t_set=cm.T_SET_C, c_set=cm.C_SET,

tau_t=1e-6, tau_c=1e-6))

radius=radius, room=MeasuredRoom())

("P4", rad, props4_scaled)]:

evap_cooling=True)

volume_term=True)

radius=rad_s)

df = pd.DataFrame(rows)

df.to_csv(os.path.join(cm.DATA_DIR, "sensitivity.csv"), index=False)

附件1、附件2 的原始数据；部分图（如敏感性龙卷风图）读取

209 def fig05_p1_verification():
210
"""Numerical vs analytical Bessel series, plus grid convergence."""
211
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
212
from verify_solver import analytical_cylinder
213
214
class ConstRoom:
215
def __init__(self, ta, ca):
216
self.ta, self.ca = ta, ca
217
218
def T_air(self, t):
219
return np.full_like(np.asarray(t, float), self.ta)
220
221
def C_air(self, t):
222
return np.full_like(np.asarray(t, float), self.ca)
223
224
alpha = 0.36 / (820.0 * 2600.0)
225
bi = cm.H_CONV * cm.R0 / 0.36
226
room = ConstRoom(60.0, 0.02)
227
times = np.array([60.0, 300.0, 900.0, 1800.0, 3600.0])
228
xi_out = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 41)
229
res = cm.simulate(800, cm.props_problem1, 3600.0, 1.0, room,
230
t_report=times, xi_report=xi_out)
231
exact = analytical_cylinder(xi_out * cm.R0, times, cm.T_INIT_C, 60.0,
232
alpha, bi)
233
err = np.abs(res["T"] - exact)
234
235
fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(11.4, 3.4))
236
lo = max(err[err > 0].min(), 1e-8) if np.any(err > 0) else 1e-8
237
m = ax[0].pcolormesh(times / 60.0, xi_out, err.T, shading="auto",
238
cmap="magma",
239
norm=plt.matplotlib.colors.LogNorm(vmin=lo,
240
vmax=err.max()))
241
fig.colorbar(m, ax=ax[0], label="绝对误差/ K", extend="both")
242
ax[0].set_xlabel("时间/ min"); ax[0].set_ylabel(r"归一化半径$\xi=r/R$")
243
ax[0].set_title(r"(a) 数值解与Bessel 级数解之差(Bi=%.2f)" % bi)
244
245
ns = [100, 200, 400, 800, 1600]
246
series = {}
247
for dt_sp in (1.0, 0.25):
248
e = []
249
for n in ns:
250
rr = cm.simulate(n, cm.props_problem1, 3600.0, dt_sp, room,
80

===== PAGE 81 =====
251
t_report=np.array([3600.0]), xi_report=xi_out)
252
ee = analytical_cylinder(xi_out * cm.R0, np.array([3600.0]),
253
cm.T_INIT_C, 60.0, alpha, bi)
254
e.append(np.abs(rr["T"] - ee).max())
255
series[dt_sp] = np.array(e)
256
for dt_sp, c, mk in ((1.0, "#c0392b", "o"), (0.25, "#1f6fb2", "s")):
257
ax[1].loglog(ns, series[dt_sp], mk + "-", color=c, lw=1.4,
258
label=r"$\Delta t=%.2f$ s" % dt_sp)
259
ref = series[0.25][0] * (np.array(ns, float) / ns[0]) ** -2.0
260
ax[1].loglog(ns, ref, "k--", lw=1.1, label=r"$\mathcal{O}(h^2)$ 参考线")
261
ax[1].set_xlabel("径向网格数$N$"); ax[1].set_ylabel("最大绝对误差/ K")
262
ax[1].set_title("(b) 空间收敛性（$t=3600$ s）")
263
ax[1].legend(fontsize=7.5)
264
265
dts = [4.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.25]
266
e_dt = []
267
for dt in dts:
268
rr = cm.simulate(800, cm.props_problem1, 3600.0, dt, room,
269
t_report=np.array([3600.0]), xi_report=xi_out)
270
ee = analytical_cylinder(xi_out * cm.R0, np.array([3600.0]),
271
cm.T_INIT_C, 60.0, alpha, bi)
272
e_dt.append(np.abs(rr["T"] - ee).max())
273
ax[2].loglog(dts, e_dt, "s-", color="#1f6fb2", lw=1.4)
274
ax[2].loglog(dts, e_dt[0] * (np.array(dts) / dts[0]) ** 2.0, "k--", lw=1.2,
275
label=r"$\mathcal{O}(\Delta t^2)$ 参考线")
276
ax[2].set_xlabel(r"时间步长$\Delta t$ / s")
277
ax[2].set_ylabel("最大绝对误差/ K")
278
ax[2].set_title("(c) 时间收敛性（$N=800$）")
279
ax[2].legend(fontsize=8)

fig.tight_layout()

return cm.savefig(fig, "fig05_verification.png", plt)

577
578 def fig14_shrink_consistency():
579
"""External check: volume conservation against the measured R(t)."""
580
d = load("p4_fields.npz")
85

===== PAGE 86 =====
581
t = d["t"] / 3600.0
582
C = d["C"]
583
xi = d["radii_cm"] / 2.0
584
w = 2.0 * xi * 0.05
585
w[0] *= 0.5
586
w[-1] *= 0.5
587
rho = 760.0 + 90.0 * C
588
rho_d = rho / (1.0 + C)
# dry matter per wet volume
589
# w integrates 2*int rho_d xi dxi, hence int rho_d xi dxi = (rho_d @ w)/2
590
integral = 0.5 * (rho_d @ w)
591
# dry mass per unit length / 2 pi, from the initial uniform state
592
M0 = cm.R0 ** 2 * ((760.0 + 90.0 * cm.C_INIT) / (1.0 + cm.C_INIT)) / 2.0
593
R_pred = np.sqrt(M0 / integral) * 100.0
594
R_meas = d["R"] * 100.0
595
596
fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(12.2, 3.5))
597
ax[0].plot(t, R_meas, lw=1.8, color="#c0392b", label="附件2 实测$R(t)$")
598
ax[0].plot(t, R_pred, lw=1.8, ls="--", color="#1f6fb2",
599
label=r"由$\rho(C)$ 与干基质量守恒反推")
600
ax[0].set_xlabel("时间/ h"); ax[0].set_ylabel("半径/ cm")
601
ax[0].set_title("(a) 收缩数据与物性公式的一致性检验")
602
ax[0].legend(fontsize=8.5)
603
604
ax[1].plot(t, 100.0 * (R_pred - R_meas) / R_meas, lw=1.6, color="#8e44ad")
605
ax[1].axhline(0, color="k", lw=0.8)
606
ax[1].set_xlabel("时间/ h"); ax[1].set_ylabel("相对偏差/ %")
607
ax[1].set_title("(b) 相对偏差：物性式与实测收缩不自洽")
608
ax[1].annotate("最大偏差%.1f%%" % np.max(np.abs(100 * (R_pred - R_meas) / R_meas)),
609
xy=(20, np.max(100 * (R_pred - R_meas) / R_meas) * 0.7),
610
fontsize=9)
611
612
ax[2].plot(t, (R_meas / R_meas[0]) ** 3, lw=1.7, color="#c0392b",
613
label="实测体积比")
614
ax[2].plot(t, (R_pred / R_pred[0]) ** 3, lw=1.7, ls="--", color="#1f6fb2",
615
label="由$\\rho(C)$ 反推的体积比")
616
ax[2].set_xlabel("时间/ h"); ax[2].set_ylabel(r"$V/V_0$")
617
ax[2].set_title("(c) 体积收缩比对比")
618
ax[2].legend(fontsize=8.5)
619
fig.tight_layout()
620
return cm.savefig(fig, "fig14_shrink_consistency.png", plt)

689 def fig16_water_balance():
690
d = load("p23_fields.npz")
691
t = d["t"]
692
xi = d["radii_cm"] / 2.0
693
w = 2.0 * xi * 0.05
694
w[0] *= 0.5
695
w[-1] *= 0.5
696
Cbar = d["C"] @ w
697
room = cm.RoomConditions()
698
Csurf = d["C"][:, -1]
699
flux = cm.HM_CONV * (Csurf - room.C_air(t))
700
W = np.pi * cm.R0 ** 2 * Cbar
# model-unit water
701
loss = W[0] - W
702
cum = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(0.5 * (flux[1:] + flux[:-1])
703
* np.diff(t))]) * 2 * np.pi * cm.R0
704
fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9.6, 3.5))
705
ax[0].plot(t / 3600.0, W * 1e3, lw=1.7, color="#1f6fb2")
706
ax[0].set_xlabel("时间/ h")
707
ax[0].set_ylabel(r"截面含水（模型单位）/ $10^{-3}$")
708
ax[0].set_title("(a) 药材内部水量的衰减")
709
ax[1].plot(t / 3600.0, cum * 1e3, lw=1.7, color="#c0392b",
710
label="表面通量累积（式(24)）")
711
ax[1].plot(t / 3600.0, loss * 1e3, "--", lw=1.5, color="#2c3e50",
712
label="内部储量变化")
87

===== PAGE 88 =====
713
ax[1].set_xlabel("时间/ h"); ax[1].set_ylabel(r"累计失水/ $10^{-3}$")
714
ax[1].set_title("(b) 全局水分守恒检验")
715
ax[1].legend(fontsize=8.5)
716
fig.tight_layout()
717
return cm.savefig(fig, "fig16_water_balance.png", plt)

906 def fig21_regime():
907
C = np.linspace(0.05, 2.55, 300)
908
Tk = np.full_like(C, 323.15)
909
D3 = cm.props_problem23(C, Tk)[3]
910
D4 = cm.props_problem4(C, Tk)[3]
90

===== PAGE 91 =====
911
bi3 = cm.HM_CONV * cm.R0 / D3
912
bi4 = cm.HM_CONV * cm.R0 / D4
913
alpha = 0.36 / (820.0 * 2600.0)
914
k3 = cm.props_problem23(C, Tk)[2]
915
cp3 = cm.props_problem23(C, Tk)[1]
916
rho3 = cm.props_problem23(C, Tk)[0]
917
a3 = k3 / (rho3 * cp3)
918
919
fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(12.2, 3.5))
920
ax[0].semilogy(C, bi3, lw=1.8, label="问题3 ($Bi_m$)")
921
ax[0].semilogy(C, bi4, lw=1.8, label="问题4 ($Bi_m$)")
922
ax[0].axhline(1, color="k", ls=":", lw=1.1)
923
ax[0].axhline(cm.H_CONV * cm.R0 / 0.36, color="#c0392b", ls="--", lw=1.4,
924
label=r"问题1 $Bi_m=3.24$")
925

926

927
ax[0].set_title("(a) 传质Biot 数：内部扩散控制")
928

929
930
tau3 = cm.R0 ** 2 / D3 / 3600.0
931
